uniform mat4 u_MVPMatrix;
attribute vec4 a_Position;
attribute vec2 a_TexCoordinate;
varying vec2 v_TexCoordinate;
void main()
v_TexCoordinate = a_TexCoordinate;
gl_Position = u_MVPMatrix * a_Position;
precision mediump float;
uniform sampler2D u_Texture;
varying vec2 v_TexCoordinate;
uniform vec4 u_Color;uniform vec3 tf;
uniform vec3 u_Tint;
void main()
vec4 tex = texture2D(u_Texture, v_TexCoordinate);
if (tex.a<0.01) discard;
float curR = (tex.r * u_Color.r)+u_Tint.r;
float curG = (tex.g * u_Color.g)+u_Tint.g;
float curB = (tex.b * u_Color.b)+u_Tint.b;
gl_FragColor = vec4(curR, curG, curB, (tex.a * u_Color.a));
precision mediump float;
uniform vec4 u_Color;
void main()
gl_FragColor = u_Color;
precision mediump float;
uniform vec4 u_Color;
uniform vec4 area;
uniform vec4 v_Color;
void main()
float xUse = gl_FragCoord.x-area.x;
float wUse = area.z;
float mixValue = xUse/wUse;
vec4 mix_color = mix(u_Color,v_Color,mixValue);
gl_FragColor = mix_color;

uniform vec4 u_Color;
uniform vec4 area;
uniform vec4 v_Color;
void main()

float hUse = area.w;
float mixValue = (yUse/hUse);
vec4 mix_color = mix(v_Color,u_Color,mixValue);
gl_FragColor = mix_color;

varying vec2 v_TexCoordinate;
uniform vec4 u_Color;
uniform vec3 u_Tint;
void main()
vec4 tex = texture2D(u_Texture, v_TexCoordinate);
if (tex.a<0.01) discard;

precision mediump float;
uniform sampler2D u_Texture;
varying vec2 v_TexCoordinate;
uniform vec4 u_Color;
uniform vec3 u_Tint;
void main()

if (tex.a<0.01) discard;

vec3 grayscale = vec3(gray);
gl_FragColor = vec4(grayscale, tex.a * u_Color.a);
